DataBase ../scada.db is opened.
StateTable was created.
Dimensions: (	StateId		INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, 	StateName 	TEXT UNIQUE NOT NULL);
BranchTable was created.
Dimensions: (	BranchId 	INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,	FromState 	INTEGER,	ToState 	INTEGER,	FOREIGN KEY(FromState) REFERENCES StateTable (StateId) ON UPDATE CASCADE ON UPDATE CASCADE ON DELETE CASCADE,	FOREIGN KEY(ToState) REFERENCES StateTable (StateId) ON UPDATE CASCADE ON DELETE CASCADE);
ConditionTable was created.
Dimensions: (	ConditionId 	INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,		BranchId 	INTEGER,		Condition 	TEXT NOT NULL,	FOREIGN KEY (BranchId) REFERENCES BranchTable (BranchId) ON UPDATE CASCADE ON DELETE CASCADE);
CalConfTable was created.
Dimensions: (	ModelNumber 	INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,		StateId 	INT, 	Type 		TEXT NOT NULL, 	Coefficient 	TEXT NOT NULL, 	FOREIGN KEY (StateId) REFERENCES StateTable (StateId) ON UPDATE CASCADE ON DELETE CASCADE);
SensorTable was created.
Dimensions: (	SensorId 	INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, 	HubID 		INT NOT NULL, 	PortNumber 	INT NOT NULL, 	SensorType 	TEXT,	UNIQUE (HubID, PortNumber));
SampleTable was created.
Dimensions: (	SampleId 	INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, 	SensorId 	INT,	TimeStamp 	TEXT NOT NULL, 	RawData 	TEXT NOT NULL, 	FOREIGN KEY (SensorId) REFERENCES SensorTable (SensorId) ON UPDATE CASCADE ON DELETE CASCADE);
CalibratedSampleTable was created.
Dimensions: (	CalId 		INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT, 	SampleId 	INTEGER, 	CalTimeStamp 	TEXT NOT NULL, 	CalVal 		INTEGER NOT NULL, 	ModelNumber 	INTEGER NOT NULL, 	MarkToDelete 	INTEGER NOT NULL, 	FOREIGN KEY (SampleId) REFERENCES SampleTable (SampleId) ON UPDATE CASCADE ON DELETE CASCADE);
SensorConfTable was created.
Dimensions: (	SensorConfId	INTEGER PRIMARY KEY AUTOINCREMENT,	StateId		INTEGER,	SensorId	INTEGER,	SampleRate	INTEGER NOT NULL,	Priority	INTEGER,	MaxValue	INTEGER,	MinValue	INTGER,	FOREIGN KEY (StateId) REFERENCES StateTable (StateId) ON UPDATE CASCADE ON DELETE CASCADE,	FOREIGN KEY (SensorId) REFERENCES SensorTable (SensorId) ON UPDATE CASCADE ON DELETE CASCADE);
